## Greenhouse Gas Pollution Pricing Act {#greenhouse-gas-pollution-pricing-act .Title-of-Act}

[S.C.]{.abbr title="Statutes of Canada"} 2018, c. 12, s. 186
:::

Assented to 2018-06-21

An Act to mitigate climate change through the pan-Canadian application
of pricing mechanisms to a broad set of greenhouse gas emission sources
and to make consequential amendments to other Acts

::: ReaderNote
\[Enacted by section 186 of chapter 12 of the Statutes of Canada, 2018,
in force on assent June 21, 2018.\]
:::
:::::

:::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
::: {.section .order}
###### Preamble {#preamble .MarginalNote}

Whereas there is broad scientific consensus that anthropogenic
greenhouse gas emissions contribute to global climate change;

Whereas recent anthropogenic emissions of greenhouse gases are at the
highest level in history and present an unprecedented risk to the
environment, including its biological diversity, to human health and
safety and to economic prosperity;

Whereas impacts of climate change, such as coastal erosion, thawing
permafrost, increases in heat waves, droughts and flooding, and related
risks to critical infrastructures and food security are already being
felt throughout Canada and are impacting Canadians, in particular the
Indigenous peoples of Canada, low-income citizens and northern, coastal
and remote communities;

Whereas Parliament recognizes that it is the responsibility of the
present generation to minimize impacts of climate change on future
generations;

Whereas the United Nations, Parliament and the scientific community have
identified climate change as an international concern which cannot be
contained within geographic boundaries;

Whereas Canada has ratified the United Nations Framework Convention on
Climate Change, done in New York on May 9, 1992, which entered into
force in 1994, and the objective of that Convention is the stabilization
of greenhouse gas concentrations in the atmosphere at a level that would
prevent dangerous anthropogenic interference with the climate system;

Whereas Canada has also ratified the Paris Agreement, done in Paris on
December 12, 2015, which entered into force in 2016, and the aims of
that Agreement include holding the increase in the global average
temperature to well below 2°C above pre-industrial levels and pursuing
efforts to limit the temperature increase to 1.5°C above pre-industrial
levels, recognizing that this would significantly reduce the risks and
impacts of climate change;

Whereas the Government of Canada is committed to achieving Canada's
Nationally Determined Contribution -- and increasing it over time --
under the Paris Agreement by taking comprehensive action to reduce
emissions across all sectors of the economy, accelerate clean economic
growth and build resilience to the impacts of climate change;

Whereas it is recognized in the Pan-Canadian Framework on Clean Growth
and Climate Change that climate change is a national problem that
requires immediate action by all governments in Canada as well as by
industry, non-governmental organizations and individual Canadians;

Whereas greenhouse gas emissions pricing is a core element of the
Pan-Canadian Framework on Clean Growth and Climate Change;

Whereas behavioural change that leads to increased energy efficiency, to
the use of cleaner energy, to the adoption of cleaner technologies and
practices and to innovation is necessary for effective action against
climate change;

Whereas the pricing of greenhouse gas emissions on a basis that
increases over time is an appropriate and efficient way to create
incentives for that behavioural change;

Whereas greenhouse gas emissions pricing reflects the "polluter pays"
principle;

Whereas some provinces are developing or have implemented greenhouse gas
emissions pricing systems;

Whereas the absence of greenhouse gas emissions pricing in some
provinces and a lack of stringency in some provincial greenhouse gas
emissions pricing systems could contribute to significant deleterious
effects on the environment, including its biological diversity, on human
health and safety and on economic prosperity;

And whereas it is necessary to create a federal greenhouse gas emissions
pricing scheme to ensure that, taking provincial greenhouse gas
emissions pricing systems into account, greenhouse gas emissions pricing
applies broadly in Canada;
:::

::: Enacts
Now, therefore, Her Majesty, by and with the advice and consent of the
Senate and House of Commons of Canada, enacts as follows:
:::

## [Short Title]{.HTitleText1} {#h-244001 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Short title

**[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** This Act may be cited as
the [Greenhouse Gas Pollution Pricing Act](/eng/acts/G-11.55).

## [Her Majesty]{.HTitleText1} {#h-244004 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Her Majesty

**[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** This Act is binding on Her
Majesty in right of Canada or a province.

## [PART 1]{.HLabel1}[Fuel Charge]{.HTitleText1} {#h-244007 .Part}

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Interpretation and General Rules of Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-244008 .Subheading}

#### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText3} {#h-244009 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** The following definitions
apply in this Part, Part 1 of Schedule 1 and Schedule 2.

[[adjustment day]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[adjustment day]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any of commencement
    day, January 1, 2019, January 1, 2020, January 1, 2021, January 1,
    2022 and any prescribed day or any day meeting prescribed
    conditions. ([date d'ajustement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[aircraft]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[aircraft]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any conveyance that is
    suitable for the transportation of individuals or goods by
    air. ([aéronef]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[assessment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[assessment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an assessment under this
    Part and includes a reassessment. ([cotisation]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[aviation gasoline]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[aviation gasoline]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a substance suitable
    for generating power by means of an aircraft engine other than a
    turbine. ([essence d'aviation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[aviation turbo fuel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[aviation turbo fuel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a substance
    suitable for generating power by means of an aircraft engine that is
    a turbine. ([carburéacteur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[bank]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[bank]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a [[bank]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}
    as defined in section 2 of the [Bank Act](/eng/acts/B-1.01) or an
    [[authorized foreign bank]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in
    that section, that is not subject to the restrictions and
    requirements referred to in subsection 524(2) of that
    Act. ([banque]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[biodiesel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[biodiesel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a particular substance

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} that is

        - [(A)]{.lawlabel} made up of mono-alkyl esters of long chain
          fatty acids derived entirely from biological matter available
          on a renewable or recurring basis, or

        - [(B)]{.lawlabel} made from plant or animal matter using a
          hydrogenation process,

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} that may contain other substances, materials
        or things, that are not described in subparagraph (i) if the
        combined proportion of those other substances, materials or
        things does not exceed 1.5% of the particular substance, and

      - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} that is suitable for generating power by
        means of a diesel engine or for use in a furnace, boiler or open
        flame burner when used

        - [(A)]{.lawlabel} on its own,

        - [(B)]{.lawlabel} after being blended with light fuel oil, or

        - [(C)]{.lawlabel} after being blended with a light fuel
          oil-like blendstock to produce light fuel oil; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed substance, material or
      thing. ([biodiesel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[biogasoline]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[biogasoline]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a particular substance

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} that is derived entirely from biological matter
        available on a renewable or recurring basis,

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} that may contain water if the proportion of
        the water does not exceed 1% of the particular substance,

      - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} that may contain other substances, materials
        or things that are not described in subparagraph (i) or (ii) if
        the combined proportion of those other substances, materials or
        things does not exceed 6% of the particular substance, and

      - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} that is suitable for generating power by means
        of an internal combustion engine other than a diesel engine when
        used

        - [(A)]{.lawlabel} on its own,

        - [(B)]{.lawlabel} after being blended with gasoline, or

        - [(C)]{.lawlabel} after being blended with a gasoline-like
          blendstock to produce gasoline; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed substance, material or
      thing. ([[bioessence]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef})

[[biomethane]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[biomethane]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a substance that is derived entirely from
      biological matter available on a renewable or recurring basis and
      that is primarily methane; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed substance, material or
      thing. ([biométhane]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[coke]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[coke]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a solid carbonaceous residue that

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is derived from low-ash, low-sulfur bituminous
      coal from which the volatile constituents are driven off by baking
      in an oven with the result that the fixed carbon and residual ash
      are fused together; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is suitable as a source of
      energy. ([coke]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[coke oven gas]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[coke oven gas]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means gas that is recovered
    from the carbonization of coal at high temperatures in a coke oven
    for the production of coke and that is suitable as a source of
    energy. ([gaz de four à coke]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[combustible waste]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[combustible waste]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} tires or asphalt shingles whether in whole or in
      part; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed substance, material or
      thing. ([déchet combustible]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[commencement day]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[commencement day]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the earliest day on
    which any of sections 17 to 26 may apply in a listed
    province. ([date de référence]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Commissioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Commissioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, except in sections 95,
    96 and 164 the Commissioner of Revenue appointed under section 25 of
    the [Canada Revenue Agency
    Act](/eng/acts/C-10.11). ([commissaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[confirmed delivery service]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[confirmed delivery service]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means certified
    or registered mail or any other delivery service that provides a
    record that a notice or document has been sent or
    delivered. ([service de messagerie]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[covered air journey]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[covered air journey]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in respect of a listed
    province means a journey by aircraft that is

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} from a particular location to another location,
      both of which are in the listed province; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed journey or a journey meeting
      prescribed conditions. ([itinéraire aérien
      assujetti]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[covered facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[covered facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a facility or
    property that is

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a [[covered facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}
      within the meaning of section 169 that is registered by the
      Minister of the Environment under section 171 other than a
      prescribed facility or property, a facility or property of a
      prescribed class or a facility or property meeting prescribed
      conditions; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed facility or property, a facility or
      property of a prescribed class or a facility or property meeting
      prescribed conditions. ([installation assujettie]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

[[covered marine journey]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[covered marine journey]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in respect of a
    listed province means a journey by vessel that is

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} from a particular location to another location,
      both of which are in the listed province; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed journey or a journey meeting
      prescribed conditions. ([itinéraire maritime
      assujetti]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[delivery]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[delivery]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in respect of fuel or in respect of
    a substance, material or thing includes, except in the definition
    [[confirmed delivery service]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} and in Division
    6, making the fuel, substance, material or thing
    available. ([livraison]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[distribution system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[distribution system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a pipe or any
    system or arrangement of pipes for the delivery or distribution of
    marketable natural gas to ultimate consumers or users. ([réseau de
    distribution]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[eligible farming activity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[eligible farming activity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the operation of eligible farming machinery on a
      farm for the purposes of farming;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the operation of eligible farming machinery for
      the purposes of going from a location at a farm to another
      location at a farm; or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed activity. ([activité agricole
      admissible]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[eligible farming machinery]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[eligible farming machinery]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means property
    that is primarily used for the purposes of farming and that is

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a farm truck or a tractor;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a vehicle not licensed to be operated on a public
      road;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an industrial machine or a stationary or portable
      engine; or

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} prescribed property;

    but does not include

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a vehicle that is an
      [[automobile]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in subsection
      248(1) of the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3);

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} property that is used for the purpose of
      providing heating or cooling to a building or similar structure;
      or

    - [(g)]{.lawlabel} prescribed property. ([machinerie agricole
      admissible]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[eligible fishing activity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[eligible fishing activity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
    operation of an eligible fishing vessel for the purposes of fishing
    or a prescribed activity. ([activité de pêche
    admissible]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[eligible fishing vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[eligible fishing vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means property that
    is primarily used for the purposes of fishing and that is a fishing
    vessel or prescribed property, but does not include prescribed
    property. ([navire de pêche admissible]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[excluded air journey]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[excluded air journey]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a journey by
    aircraft that

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} begins or ends in a listed province other than

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a covered air journey, or

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed journey or a journey meeting
        prescribed conditions; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is a prescribed journey or a journey meeting
      prescribed conditions. ([itinéraire aérien exclu]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

[[excluded marine journey]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[excluded marine journey]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a journey by
    vessel that

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} begins or ends in a listed province other than

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a covered marine journey, or

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed journey or a journey meeting
        prescribed conditions; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is a prescribed journey or a journey meeting
      prescribed conditions. ([itinéraire maritime
      exclu]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[farmer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[farmer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person that carries on a
    farming business with a reasonable expectation of
    profit. ([agriculteur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[farming]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[farming]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes tillage of the soil,
    livestock raising or exhibiting, maintaining of horses for racing,
    raising of poultry, fur farming, dairy farming, fruit growing and
    the keeping of bees, but does not include an office or employment
    under a person engaged in the business of
    farming. ([agriculture]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[fisher]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[fisher]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person that carries on a
    fishing business with a reasonable expectation of
    profit. ([pêcheur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[fishing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[fishing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes fishing for or catching
    shellfish, crustaceans and marine animals but does not include an
    office or employment under a person engaged in the business of
    fishing. ([pêche]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[fuel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[fuel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a substance, material or thing set out in column
      2 of any table in Schedule 2, other than

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} combustible waste,

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a substance, material or thing that is
        prepackaged in a factory sealed container of 10 L or less, or

      - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed substance, material or thing;
        and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed substance, material or
      thing. ([combustible]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[gas liquids]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[gas liquids]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a mixture in gaseous or
    liquid form that consists of two or more of ethane, propane, butane
    or pentanes plus and that

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is separated, as a result of processing, from
      natural gas or crude oil for the first time;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has not been

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} analyzed to assess composition, or

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} processed into separate identifiable fuels;
        and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is not a mixture of ethane, propane, butane or
      pentanes plus created after the ethane, propane, butane or
      pentanes plus have been processed into separate identifiable fuels
      and subsequently remixed into a blend of one or more of the
      fuels. ([liquides de gaz]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[gasoline]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[gasoline]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a substance, including
    biogasoline, that is suitable for generating power by means of an
    internal combustion engine other than a diesel engine and that is
    not any other type of fuel. ([essence]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[heavy fuel oil]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[heavy fuel oil]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a substance that is not
    petroleum coke and that is made up of a distillate or a residual of
    crude oil and that has a viscosity greater than 14 centistokes at
    50°C. ([mazout lourd]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[high heat value coal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[high heat value coal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means coal with a
    heating value exceeding 27,000 kJ/kg. ([charbon à pouvoir
    calorifique supérieur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[import]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[import]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means import into
    Canada. ([importation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[interjurisdictional air carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[interjurisdictional air carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in respect
    of a type of fuel means a person that, in the course of providing a
    commercial service of transporting individuals or goods by aircraft,
    uses fuel of that type, in the ordinary course of business, in
    excluded air journeys. ([transporteur aérien entre
    administrations]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[interjurisdictional marine carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[interjurisdictional marine carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in
    respect of a type of fuel means a person that, in the course of
    providing a commercial service of transporting individuals or goods
    by vessel, uses fuel of that type, in the ordinary course of
    business, in excluded marine journeys. ([transporteur maritime entre
    administrations]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[interjurisdictional rail carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[interjurisdictional rail carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in respect
    of a type of fuel means a person that uses fuel of that type, in the
    ordinary course of business, in a listed province in the course of
    providing a commercial service of transporting individuals or goods
    by rail

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} from one province to another province; or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} between a place in Canada and a place outside
      Canada. ([transporteur ferroviaire entre
      administrations]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[journey]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[journey]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the transportation of
    individuals or goods by aircraft or vessel from a particular
    location to another location where the aircraft or vessel is next
    stopped if any of the following activities occurs at the particular
    location and if any of the following activities occurs at the other
    location:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} individuals embark or disembark the aircraft or
      vessel;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} goods are loaded onto or removed from the
      aircraft or vessel; or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the aircraft or vessel is stopped to allow for
      its servicing or refuelling or for emergency or safety
      purposes. ([itinéraire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[judge]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[judge]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in respect of any matter, means a
    judge of a superior court having jurisdiction in the province in
    which the matter arises or a judge of the Federal
    Court. ([juge]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[kerosene]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[kerosene]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a light petroleum distillate
    that meets the requirements of the National Standard of Canada
    CAN/CGSB-3.3, Kerosene, as amended from time to time, but does not
    include aviation turbo fuel. ([kérosène]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[light fuel oil]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[light fuel oil]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a substance that

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is made up of

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a distillate or a residual of crude oil that
        has a viscosity not greater than 14 centistokes at 50°C, or

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} biodiesel;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is suitable for generating power by means of a
      diesel engine or is suitable for use in a furnace, boiler or open
      flame burner; and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is not butane, ethane, gas liquids, aviation
      turbo fuel, kerosene, naphtha, propane, pentanes plus or still
      gas. ([mazout léger]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[listed province]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[listed province]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a province or area
    listed in Part 1 of Schedule 1. ([province
    assujettie]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[locomotive]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[locomotive]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes self-propelled on-track
    railway equipment but does not include vehicles that are suitable
    for movement both on and off lines of
    railway. ([locomotive]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[low heat value coal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[low heat value coal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means coal with a
    heating value of 27,000 kJ/kg or less. ([charbon à pouvoir
    calorifique inférieur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[marketable natural gas]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[marketable natural gas]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means natural gas
    that consists of at least 90% methane and that meets the
    specifications for pipeline transport and sale for general
    distribution to the public. ([gaz naturel
    commercialisable]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[methanol]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[methanol]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} does not include methanol derived
    entirely from biological matter available on a renewable or
    recurring basis. ([méthanol]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Minister of National
    Revenue. ([ministre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[mixture]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[mixture]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a substance, material or thing
    that is a combination of two or more types of
    fuel. ([mélange]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[naphtha]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[naphtha]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a refined or partially refined
    petroleum fraction with an approximate boiling temperature between
    50°C and 204°C other than aviation gasoline, aviation turbo fuel,
    gasoline, heavy fuel oil, kerosene, light fuel oil or petroleum
    coke. ([naphta]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[natural gas]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[natural gas]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a combination of
    natural gas and biomethane but does not include still gas. ([gaz
    naturel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[net charge]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[net charge]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} for a reporting period of a
    person means the amount determined for the reporting period of the
    person under subsection 71(2). ([redevance nette]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[non-covered activity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[non-covered activity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an activity in
    respect of which fuel

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is used

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} as a raw material in an industrial process that
        produces another fuel or another substance, material or thing,

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} as a solvent or diluent in the production or
        transport of crude bitumen or another substance, material or
        thing, or

      - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in prescribed circumstances; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is not put into a fuel system that produces heat
      or energy and is not burned or flared. ([activité non
      assujettie]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[non-marketable natural gas]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[non-marketable natural gas]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means natural gas
    other than marketable natural gas. ([gaz naturel non
    commercialisable]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, except in sections 90, 138
    and 160,

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a person who is appointed or employed in the
      administration or enforcement of this Part; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} with respect to imported goods that have not been
      released under the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), an
      [[officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in subsection 2(1) of
      that Act. ([préposé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[pentanes plus]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[pentanes plus]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a substance that is
    obtained from the production or processing of raw gas, condensate or
    crude oil, that is not any other type of fuel and that is

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} pentane;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} hydrocarbons heavier than pentane; or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a combination of pentane and heavier
      hydrocarbons. ([pentanes plus]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an individual, a partnership, a
    corporation, the estate or succession of a deceased individual, a
    trust, a joint venture, a government or a body that is a society, a
    union, a club, an association, a commission or another organization
    of any kind. ([personne]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[personal representative]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[personal representative]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} of a deceased
    individual or the estate or succession of a deceased individual,
    means the executor of the individual's will, the liquidator of the
    individual's succession, the administrator of the estate or any
    person that is responsible under the appropriate law for the proper
    collection, administration, disposition and distribution of the
    assets of the estate or succession. ([représentant
    personnel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[petroleum coke]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[petroleum coke]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a carbonaceous solid produced from an oil
      refinery coke unit or an oil or bitumen upgrader coker unit;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a carbonaceous solid produced from a cracking
      process, including coking, fluid coking, flexicoking and delayed
      coking; or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any substance commonly referred to as "green
      coke" or "fuel grade coke". ([coke de pétrole]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

[[prescribed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[prescribed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a form or the manner of filing a
      form, authorized by the Minister;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the information to be given on or
      with a form, specified by the Minister;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of the manner of making or filing an
      election, authorized by the Minister; and

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, prescribed by regulation or
      determined in accordance with rules prescribed by
      regulation. ([Version anglaise
      seulement]{.DefinitionOtherLangOnly})

[[produce]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[produce]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of fuel, to obtain
    fuel or bring fuel into existence by any method or process including

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} mining, extracting, removing or otherwise
      obtaining from the earth;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} manufacturing, synthesizing, refining or
      blending; or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} using any means of altering the chemical or
      physical properties of a substance, material or
      thing. ([production]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[qualifying aviation fuel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[qualifying aviation fuel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a type of
    fuel that is aviation gasoline, aviation turbo fuel or a prescribed
    type of fuel. ([combustible d'aviation admissible]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[qualifying farming fuel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[qualifying farming fuel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a type of fuel
    that is gasoline, light fuel oil or a prescribed type of
    fuel. ([combustible agricole admissible]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[qualifying fishing fuel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[qualifying fishing fuel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a type of fuel
    that is gasoline, light fuel oil or a prescribed type of
    fuel. ([combustible de pêche admissible]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[qualifying marine fuel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[qualifying marine fuel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a type of fuel
    that is heavy fuel oil, light fuel oil, marketable natural gas or a
    prescribed type of fuel. ([combustible maritime
    admissible]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[qualifying motive fuel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[qualifying motive fuel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a type of fuel
    that is gasoline, light fuel oil, marketable natural gas, propane or
    a prescribed type of fuel. ([combustible moteur
    admissible]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[qualifying rail fuel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[qualifying rail fuel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a type of fuel
    that is light fuel oil, marketable natural gas or a prescribed type
    of fuel. ([combustible ferroviaire admissible]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[rate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[rate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in respect of a type of fuel, or in
    respect of combustible waste, for a listed province at a particular
    time means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} unless paragraph (b) applies, the rate set out in
      column 5 of the table in Schedule 2 that is applicable for the
      period of time that includes the particular time and that is
      opposite

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} that type of fuel or combustible waste, as the
        case may be, set out in column 2 of that table, and

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the name of that listed province set out in
        column 4 of that table; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
      conditions are met, the prescribed rate or the rate determined in
      prescribed manner that is applicable at the particular time for
      that listed province and for that type of fuel or combustible
      waste, as the case may be. ([taux]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[record]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[record]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any material on which
    representations, in any form, of information or concepts are
    recorded or marked and that is capable of being read or understood
    by a person or a computer system or other
    device. ([registre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[registered air carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[registered air carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in respect of a type
    of fuel means a person that is registered under Division 4 of this
    Part as an air carrier in respect of that type of
    fuel. ([transporteur aérien inscrit]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[registered distributor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[registered distributor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in respect of a type
    of fuel means a person that is registered under Division 4 of this
    Part as a distributor in respect of that type of
    fuel. ([distributeur inscrit]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[registered emitter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[registered emitter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person that is
    registered under Division 4 of this Part as an emitter. ([émetteur
    inscrit]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[registered importer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[registered importer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in respect of a type of
    fuel means a person that is registered under Division 4 of this Part
    as an importer in respect of that type of fuel. ([importateur
    inscrit]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[registered marine carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[registered marine carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in respect of a
    type of fuel means a person that is registered under Division 4 of
    this Part as a marine carrier in respect of that type of
    fuel. ([transporteur maritime inscrit]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[registered rail carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[registered rail carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in respect of a type
    of fuel means a person that is registered under Division 4 of this
    Part as a rail carrier in respect of that type of
    fuel. ([transporteur ferroviaire inscrit]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[registered road carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[registered road carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in respect of a type
    of fuel means a person that is registered under Division 4 of this
    Part as a road carrier in respect of that type of
    fuel. ([transporteur routier inscrit]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[registered specified air carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[registered specified air carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in respect
    of a type of fuel means a person that is registered under Division 4
    of this Part as a specified air carrier in respect of that type of
    fuel. ([transporteur aérien désigné inscrit]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[registered specified marine carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[registered specified marine carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in
    respect of a type of fuel means a person that is registered under
    Division 4 of this Part as a specified marine carrier in respect of
    that type of fuel. ([transporteur maritime désigné
    inscrit]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[registered specified rail carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[registered specified rail carrier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in respect
    of a type of fuel means a person that is registered under Division 4
    of this Part as a specified rail carrier in respect of that type of
    fuel. ([transporteur ferroviaire désigné inscrit]{.DefinedTermLink
    lang="fr"})

[[registered user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[registered user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in respect of a type of fuel
    or in respect of combustible waste means a person that is registered
    under Division 4 of this Part as a user in respect of that type of
    fuel or in respect of combustible waste. ([utilisateur
    inscrit]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[reporting period]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[reporting period]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} of a person means the
    reporting period of the person as determined under section
    68. ([période de déclaration]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[specified commercial vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[specified commercial vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vehicle

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that is used to provide commercial transportation
      of individuals or goods by road

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} from one province to another province, or

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} from a particular location to another location
        if one location is in Canada and one location is outside Canada;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} has two axles and a gross vehicle weight
        exceeding 11,797 kg,

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} has three or more axles regardless of weight,
        or

      - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} when combined with the trailer with which it
        is used, has a gross vehicle weight exceeding 11,797 kg; and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} that is not a recreational vehicle, including a
      motor home, bus or pickup truck with attached camper, if used
      solely for a particular individual's personal use or enjoyment or
      the personal use or enjoyment of any other individual at the
      particular individual's expense. ([véhicule commercial
      désigné]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[still gas]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[still gas]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means gas suitable for use in an
    oil refinery that is produced as a result of distillation, cracking,
    reforming or other oil refining processes. ([gaz de
    distillation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[supply tank]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[supply tank]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a receptacle of a vehicle
    in which fuel is held for use in the operation of 

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the vehicle;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an auxiliary component of the vehicle; or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an auxiliary component of another vehicle, if the
      other vehicle is attached to the vehicle. ([réservoir
      d'alimentation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[use]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[use]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes flaring but does not include
    venting. ([utilisation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any conveyance that is
    suitable for the transportation of individuals or goods by water,
    land or air. ([véhicule]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any conveyance that is suitable
    for the transportation of individuals or goods by
    water. ([navire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Meaning of administration or enforcement
of this Part

- **[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  greater certainty, a reference in this Part to the administration or
  enforcement of this Part includes the collection of any amount payable
  under this Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations under this Part

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, a reference to "this Part" in
  section 3, subsection 4(1) or sections 5 to 168 is to be read as a
  reference to "this Part or regulations made under this Part".

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Covered facility of a person

**[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of this
Part, a covered facility is a covered facility of a person if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a covered facility certificate in respect of the
  covered facility has been issued to the person by the Minister of the
  Environment under section 171; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person is a prescribed person, a person of a
  prescribed class or a person meeting prescribed conditions in respect
  of the covered facility.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Arm's length

- **[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the
  purposes of this Part,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} related persons are deemed not to deal with each
    other at arm's length; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is a question of fact whether persons not
    related to each other are, at any particular time, dealing with each
    other at arm's length.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Related persons

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Part, persons are related to
  each other if they are related persons within the meaning of
  subsection 6(2) of the [Excise Act, 2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exclusive economic zone and continental
shelf

**[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** For greater certainty, an
area, for the purposes of this Part, may include all or part of the
exclusive economic zone of Canada or the continental shelf of Canada.

#### [General Rules of Application]{.HTitleText3} {#h-244223 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determining quantities --- litres

- **[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Unless
  any of subsections (5), (6) and (8) apply, for the purpose of
  determining a quantity of fuel of a certain type under this Part, if
  the rate in respect of that type of fuel is expressed in \$/litre, the
  quantity of fuel is the number of litres that the fuel would occupy at
  15°C.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determining quantities --- cubic metres

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Unless subsection (7) or (8) applies, for the purpose
  of determining a quantity of fuel of a certain type under this Part,
  if the rate in respect of that type of fuel is expressed in \$/cubic
  metre, the quantity of fuel is the number of cubic metres that the
  fuel would occupy at 15°C and 101.325 kPa.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determining quantities --- coal

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Unless subsection (8) applies, for the purpose of
  determining a quantity of high heat value coal or low heat value coal
  under this Part, the quantity of coal is the weight of the coal
  measured in tonnes and normalized to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 7.7% moisture by weight in the case of high heat
    value coal; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 19% moisture by weight in the case of low heat
    value coal.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determining quantities --- coke

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Unless subsection (8) applies, for the purpose of
  determining a quantity of coke under this Part, the quantity of coke
  is the weight of the coke measured in tonnes and, if a moisture
  content is prescribed, normalized to the prescribed moisture content.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Gasoline with proportion of biogasoline
  exceeding 10%

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Unless subsection (8) applies, if a quantity of
  gasoline contains a particular proportion of biogasoline (expressed as
  a percentage) that exceeds 10%, the quantity of gasoline is deemed,
  for the purpose of this Part, to be the number of litres determined by
  the formula

  ::: {#244237 .Subsection}
  A × (100% -- B)/95%

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the number of litres that the gasoline would occupy at 15°C;
      and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the particular proportion.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Light fuel oil with proportion of
  biodiesel exceeding 5%

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Unless subsection (8) applies, if a quantity of light
  fuel oil contains a particular proportion of biodiesel (expressed as a
  percentage) that exceeds 5%, the quantity of light fuel oil is deemed,
  for the purpose of this Part, to be the number of litres determined by
  the formula

  ::: {#244243 .Subsection}
  A × (100% -- B)/98%

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the number of litres that the light fuel oil would occupy at
      15°C; and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the particular proportion.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Natural gas that contains biomethane

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Unless subsection (8) applies, if a quantity of
  marketable natural gas or non-marketable natural gas contains a
  particular proportion of biomethane (expressed as a percentage), for
  the purpose of this Part, the quantity of marketable natural gas or
  non-marketable natural gas is deemed to be the number of cubic metres
  determined by the formula

  ::: {#244249 .Subsection}
  A × (100% -- B)

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the number of cubic metres that the marketable natural gas or
      non-marketable natural gas would occupy at 15°C and 101.325 kPa;
      and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the particular proportion.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determining quantities --- prescribed
  type of fuel

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of determining a quantity under this
  Part of a prescribed type of fuel, the quantity of fuel of that type
  is determined in prescribed manner if prescribed conditions are met.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determining quantities

**[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** Any determination of a
quantity of fuel under this Part is to be made in a manner satisfactory
to the Minister.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fuel brought into a listed province

- **[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of this Part, if a particular person is transporting fuel
  on behalf of another person and the fuel is, at a particular time,
  brought into a listed province in the course of being transported to a
  location in the listed province, the other person and not the
  particular person is deemed to have brought the fuel into the listed
  province at the particular time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fuel removed from a listed province

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Part, if a particular person
  is transporting fuel on behalf of another person and the fuel is, at a
  particular time, removed from a listed province in the course of being
  transported to a location outside the listed province, the other
  person and not the particular person is deemed to have removed the
  fuel from the listed province at the particular time.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fuel in transit through a listed province

**[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of this
Part, if a person at a particular time brings a quantity of fuel into a
listed province from a place in Canada, if the fuel is being brought
into the listed province in the course of the transportation of the fuel
to a place outside the listed province, if the fuel is transported
without being stored in the listed province (otherwise than in a manner
that is solely incidental to the transportation) and if the person is a
registered emitter or is registered under Division 4 of this Part in
respect of that type of fuel otherwise than only as a road carrier, then
the fuel is deemed not to have been brought into the listed province at
the particular time.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fuel imported in a listed province

**[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of this
Part, if a person at a particular time imports a quantity of fuel at a
location in a listed province, if the fuel is imported in the course of
the transportation of the fuel to a place outside the listed province,
if the fuel is transported without being stored in the listed province
(otherwise than in a manner that is solely incidental to the
transportation) and if the person is a registered emitter or is
registered under Division 4 of this Part in respect of that type of fuel
otherwise than only as a road carrier, then the fuel is deemed not to
have been imported at a location in the listed province at the
particular time.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Importer

**[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of this
Part, the person that is considered to import fuel is the importer,
owner or other person that is liable under the [Customs
Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) to pay duty levied under section 20 of the
[Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) on the fuel or that would be liable
to pay that duty on the fuel if the fuel were subject to that duty.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delivery of marketable natural gas ---
distribution system

**[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of this
Part, if marketable natural gas is delivered to a particular person by
means of a distribution system, the person that is considered to deliver
the marketable natural gas is

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} unless paragraph (b) applies, the person that
  measures, on a regular basis and for the purpose of billing the
  particular person or providing the particular person's billing
  information to a third party, the particular person's consumption or
  usage of marketable natural gas that is delivered by means of the
  distribution system; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
  conditions are met, the person that is a prescribed person, a person
  of a prescribed class or a person meeting prescribed conditions.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Substance marketed as fuel

**[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15 .sectionLabel}** If a substance, material
or thing is not fuel but is sold, represented or marketed as fuel of a
particular type, the substance, material or thing is deemed, for the
purposes of this Part, to be fuel of the particular type, except if the
substance, material or thing is prepackaged in a factory sealed
container of 10 L or less, is combustible waste or is a prescribed
substance, material or thing.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Mixtures

- **[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  mixture is deemed to be fuel of the type that is present in the
  highest proportion in the mixture.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prescribed mixtures

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), if prescribed conditions are
  met in respect of a mixture, the mixture is deemed to be fuel of a
  prescribed type.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} This section does not apply to a substance, material
  or thing that would be fuel in the absence of subsections (1) and (2).

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Application of Charge]{.HTitleText2} {#h-244281 .Subheading}

#### [SUBDIVISION A]{.HLabel3}[General Application of Charge to Fuel and Combustible Waste]{.HTitleText3} {#h-244282 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge --- delivery by registered
distributor

- **[[17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Part, a particular
  registered distributor in respect of a type of fuel that delivers, at
  a particular time, fuel of that type in a listed province to another
  person must pay to Her Majesty in right of Canada a charge in respect
  of the fuel and the listed province in the amount determined under
  section 40. The charge becomes payable at the particular time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge not payable

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The charge under subsection (1) is not payable if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the particular registered distributor delivers the
    fuel in the listed province to another person that is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in respect of that type of fuel, a registered
      distributor, a registered specified air carrier, a registered
      specified marine carrier, a registered specified rail carrier or a
      registered user,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a registered emitter,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a farmer, if the fuel is a qualifying farming
      fuel,

    - [(iii.1)]{.lawlabel} a fisher, if the fuel is a qualifying fishing
      fuel and the listed province is prescribed, or

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed person, a person of a prescribed
      class or a person meeting prescribed conditions, if prescribed
      circumstances exist; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an exemption certificate applies in respect of the
    delivery in accordance with section 36.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge not payable --- ships' stores

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The charge under subsection (1) is not payable if the
  fuel is, in accordance with the [Ships' Stores
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-40), designated as ships' stores
  for use on board a conveyance of a class prescribed under those
  regulations.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge --- use by registered distributor

- **[[18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-18
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Part, every
  registered distributor in respect of a type of fuel that uses, at a
  particular time, fuel of that type in a listed province must pay to
  Her Majesty in right of Canada a charge in respect of the fuel and the
  listed province in the amount determined under section 40. The charge
  becomes payable at the particular time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed use --- supply tank

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), if a registered
  distributor in respect of a type of fuel transfers fuel of that type
  at a particular time into a supply tank of a vehicle (other than a
  specified commercial vehicle) of the registered distributor at a
  particular location and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the particular location is in a listed province,
    the registered distributor is deemed to use the fuel at the
    particular time in the listed province; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the particular location is not in a listed
    province, the registered distributor is deemed to use the fuel at
    the particular time otherwise than in a listed province.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (2) does not apply if the registered
  distributor is also a registered emitter and the particular location
  is a covered facility of the registered distributor.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge not payable --- used at a
  covered facility

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The charge under subsection (1) is not payable to the
  extent that the fuel is used by the registered distributor at a
  covered facility of the registered distributor if the registered
  distributor is also a registered emitter.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge not payable --- used in a
  non-covered activity

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The charge under subsection (1) is not payable to the
  extent that the fuel is used in a non-covered activity.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge --- bringing into a listed
province

- **[[19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-19
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Part, every person
  that brings, at a particular time, fuel into a listed province from a
  place in Canada must pay to Her Majesty in right of Canada a charge in
  respect of the fuel and the listed province in the amount determined
  under section 40 if the person is a registered emitter or is, in
  respect of that type of fuel, a registered user, a registered
  importer, a registered air carrier, a registered marine carrier or a
  registered rail carrier. The charge becomes payable at the particular
  time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge --- importation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Part, every person that imports, at a
  particular time, fuel at a location in a listed province must pay to
  Her Majesty in right of Canada a charge in respect of the fuel and the
  listed province in the amount determined under section 40 if the
  person is a registered emitter or is, in respect of that type of fuel,
  a registered user, a registered importer, a registered air carrier, a
  registered marine carrier or a registered rail carrier. The charge
  becomes payable at the particular time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) and (2) do not apply, in respect of a
  type of fuel, to a person that is a registered distributor in respect
  of that type of fuel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge not payable --- supply tanks

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The charge under subsection (1) or (2) is not payable
  if the fuel is brought in or imported in a supply tank of a vehicle
  and the fuel is for use in the operation of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the vehicle;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an auxiliary component of the vehicle; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an auxiliary component of another vehicle attached
    to the vehicle.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (4) does not apply to fuel that is brought
  into a listed province, or imported at a location in a listed
  province, by a person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the fuel is transported in a supply tank of a
    specified commercial vehicle of the person, the fuel is a type of
    qualifying motive fuel and the person

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is a registered emitter or is, in respect of that
      type of fuel, a registered importer or a registered user, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is required to be registered under Division 4 of
      this Part as a road carrier in respect of that type of fuel but is
      not so registered;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the fuel is brought in or imported in a supply
    tank of a locomotive and the person is an interjurisdictional rail
    carrier in respect of that type of fuel and is not, in respect of
    that type of fuel, registered as an air carrier, marine carrier or
    rail carrier;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} to the extent that the fuel is brought in or
    imported in a supply tank of an aircraft for use in a covered air
    journey in respect of the listed province, if the person is an
    interjurisdictional air carrier in respect of that type of fuel and
    is not, in respect of that type of fuel, registered as an air
    carrier, marine carrier or rail carrier; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} to the extent that the fuel is brought in or
    imported in a supply tank of a vessel for use in a covered marine
    journey in respect of the listed province, if the person is an
    interjurisdictional marine carrier in respect of that type of fuel
    and is not, in respect of that type of fuel, registered as an air
    carrier, marine carrier or rail carrier.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

- **[[20]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-20 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} This
  section does not apply, in respect of a type of fuel, to a person that
  is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a registered distributor in respect of that type of
    fuel;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a registered importer in respect of that type of
    fuel;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a registered specified air carrier or registered
    air carrier in respect of that type of fuel;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a registered specified marine carrier or registered
    marine carrier in respect of that type of fuel;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a registered specified rail carrier or registered
    rail carrier in respect of that type of fuel;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a registered emitter; or

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a registered user in respect of that type of fuel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge --- bringing into a listed
  province

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Part, every person that brings, at a
  particular time, fuel into a listed province from a place in Canada
  must pay to Her Majesty in right of Canada a charge in respect of the
  fuel and the listed province in the amount determined under
  section 40. The charge becomes payable at the particular time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge --- importation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Part, every person that imports, at a
  particular time, fuel at a location in a listed province must pay to
  Her Majesty in right of Canada a charge in respect of the fuel and the
  listed province in the amount determined under section 40. The charge
  becomes payable at the particular time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of [Customs
  Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6)

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The charge under subsection (3) is to be paid and
  collected under the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), and interest and
  penalties are to be imposed, calculated, paid and collected under that
  Act, as if the charge were a customs duty levied on the fuel under the
  [Customs Tariff](/eng/acts/C-54.011) and, for those purposes, the
  [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6), with any modification that the
  circumstances require, applies subject to this Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge not payable --- small quantities

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A charge under subsection (2) or (3) is not payable
  if the fuel is brought in or imported otherwise than in a supply tank
  of a vehicle, the fuel is gasoline, kerosene, light fuel oil or
  propane and the quantity of the fuel does not exceed 200 L.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge not payable --- supply tanks

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A charge under subsection (2) or (3) is not payable
  if the fuel is brought in or imported in a supply tank of a vehicle
  and the fuel is for use in the operation of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the vehicle;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an auxiliary component of the vehicle; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an auxiliary component of another vehicle attached
    to the vehicle.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- supply tanks

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (6) does not apply

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the person is required to be registered under
    Division 4 of this Part as a specified air carrier or air carrier in
    respect of that type of fuel but is not so registered;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the person is required to be registered under
    Division 4 of this Part as a specified marine carrier or marine
    carrier in respect of that type of fuel but is not so registered;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the person is required to be registered under
    Division 4 of this Part as a specified rail carrier or rail carrier
    in respect that type of fuel but is not so registered;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the fuel is in a supply tank of a specified
    commercial vehicle and the person is required to be registered under
    Division 4 of this Part as a road carrier in respect of that type
    fuel but is not so registered;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if the person is an interjurisdictional rail
    carrier in respect of that type of fuel and the fuel is brought in
    or imported in a supply tank of a locomotive;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} to the extent that the fuel is brought in or
    imported in a supply tank of an aircraft for use in a covered air
    journey in respect of the listed province, if the person is an
    interjurisdictional air carrier in respect of that type of fuel; or

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} to the extent that the fuel is brought in or
    imported in a supply tank of a vessel for use in a covered marine
    journey in respect of the listed province, if the person is an
    interjurisdictional marine carrier in respect of that type of fuel.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge --- production

- **[[21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Part, a person that
  produces at a particular time fuel in a listed province must pay to
  Her Majesty in right of Canada a charge in respect of the fuel and the
  listed province in the amount determined under section 40 unless the
  person is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a registered distributor in respect of that type of
    fuel;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a registered specified air carrier in respect of
    that type of fuel;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a registered specified marine carrier in respect of
    that type of fuel;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a registered specified rail carrier in respect of
    that type of fuel; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed person, a person of a prescribed class
    or a person meeting prescribed conditions.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When charge payable

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The charge under subsection (1) becomes payable at
  the particular time referred to in that subsection.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge --- diversion from covered
facility

- **[[22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-22
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Part, if at any time
  fuel is delivered in a listed province by a registered distributor in
  respect of that type of fuel to a person and an exemption certificate
  applies in respect of the delivery in accordance with section 36, the
  person must pay to Her Majesty in right of Canada a charge in respect
  of the fuel and the listed province in the amount determined under
  section 40 to the extent that the fuel is removed, at a later time,
  from a covered facility of the person in the listed province.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge --- diversion of fuel intended
  for use at covered facility

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Part, if at any time fuel is
  delivered in a listed province by a registered distributor in respect
  of that type of fuel to a person at a location that is not a covered
  facility of the person, if an exemption certificate applies in respect
  of the delivery in accordance with section 36 and if the certificate
  includes the declaration referred to in subparagraph 36(1)(b)(v), the
  person must, unless a charge under subsection (1) applies in respect
  of the fuel and the listed province, pay to Her Majesty in right of
  Canada a charge in respect of the fuel and the listed province in the
  amount determined under section 40 to the extent that, at a later
  time, the fuel is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} used by the person in the listed province otherwise
    than at a covered facility; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} delivered by the person to another person unless
    the other person is a registered distributor in respect of that type
    of fuel and an exemption certificate applies in respect of the
    delivery in accordance with section 36.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge --- diversion from covered
  facility following a rebate

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Part, if a quantity of fuel is, at
  any time, brought to a covered facility of a person in a listed
  province and a rebate under section 44 in respect of the fuel and the
  listed province is payable to the person, then the person must pay to
  Her Majesty in right of Canada a charge in respect of the fuel and the
  listed province in the amount determined under section 40, to the
  extent the fuel is removed, at a later time, from a covered facility
  of the person in the listed province.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When charge payable

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The charge under any of subsections (1) to (3)
  becomes payable at the later time referred to in whichever of those
  subsections applies.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge not payable

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The charge under any of subsections (1) to (3) is not
  payable by the person if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the fuel is removed from the covered facility of
    the person in the listed province and

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is delivered by the person to a registered
      distributor in respect of that type of fuel and an exemption
      certificate applies in respect of the delivery in accordance with
      section 36, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is brought to another covered facility of the
      person in the listed province;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} at the later time referred to in whichever of those
    subsections is applicable, the person

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is not a registered emitter, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is a registered distributor in respect of that
      type of fuel; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
    conditions are met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge --- ceasing to be a covered
  facility

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Part, if at any time fuel is
  delivered to a person in a listed province by a registered distributor
  in respect of that type of fuel and an exemption certificate applies
  in respect of the delivery in accordance with section 36, the person
  must pay to Her Majesty in right of Canada a charge in respect of the
  fuel and the listed province in the amount determined under section 40
  to the extent that the fuel, at a later time, is held by the person
  at, or is in transit to, a facility or property of the person in the
  listed province that ceases, at the later time, to be a covered
  facility of the person in the listed province. The charge becomes
  payable at the later time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge --- ceasing to be a covered
  facility following a rebate

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Part, if a quantity of fuel is, at
  any time, brought to a covered facility of a person in a listed
  province and a rebate under section 44 in respect of the fuel and the
  listed province is payable to the person, then the person must pay to
  Her Majesty in right of Canada a charge in respect of the fuel and the
  listed province in the amount determined under section 40, to the
  extent that the fuel, at a later time, is held by the person at, or is
  in transit to, a facility or property of the person in the listed
  province that ceases, at the later time, to be a covered facility of
  the person in the listed province. The charge becomes payable at the
  later time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge not payable

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} The charge under subsection (6) or (7) is not payable
  if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at the later time referred to in whichever of those
    subsections is applicable, the person

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is not a registered emitter, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is a registered distributor in respect of that
      type of fuel; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
    conditions are met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge --- ceasing to be an emitter

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Part, if at any time fuel is
  delivered to a person in a listed province by a registered distributor
  in respect of that type of fuel, if an exemption certificate applies
  in respect of the delivery in accordance with section 36 and if the
  Minister, at a later time, cancels the person's registration as an
  emitter, the person must pay to Her Majesty in right of Canada a
  charge in respect of the fuel and the listed province in the amount
  determined under section 40 to the extent that, at the later time, the
  fuel is held by the person at, or is in transit to, a facility or
  property that was a covered facility of the person in the listed
  province immediately before the later time. The charge becomes payable
  at the later time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge --- ceasing to be an emitter
  following a rebate

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Part, if a quantity of fuel is, at
  any time, brought to a covered facility of a person in a listed
  province, if a rebate under section 44 in respect of the fuel and the
  listed province is payable to the person and if the Minister, at a
  later time, cancels the person's registration as an emitter, then the
  person must pay to Her Majesty in right of Canada a charge in respect
  of the fuel and the listed province in the amount determined under
  section 40 to the extent that, at the later time, the fuel is held by
  the person at, or is in transit to, a facility or property that was a
  covered facility of the person in the listed province immediately
  before the later time. The charge becomes payable at the later time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge not payable

  [(11)]{.lawlabel} The charge under subsection (9) or (10) is not
  payable if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at the later time referred to in whichever of those
    subsections is applicable, the person is a registered distributor in
    respect of that type of fuel; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
    conditions are met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge not payable

  [(12)]{.lawlabel} The charge under subsection (1), (2), (6) or (9) is
  not payable if a charge is payable under section 37 in respect of the
  fuel.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge --- diversion by registered user

- **[[23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-23
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Part, if at any time
  fuel is delivered in a listed province by a registered distributor in
  respect of that type of fuel to a particular person that is a
  registered user in respect of that type of fuel and an exemption
  certificate applies in respect of the delivery in accordance with
  section 36, the particular person must pay to Her Majesty in right of
  Canada a charge in respect of the fuel and the listed province in the
  amount determined under section 40 to the extent that the fuel is, at
  a later time,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} used by the particular person in the listed
    province otherwise than in a non-covered activity; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} delivered by the particular person to another
    person unless the other person is a registered distributor in
    respect of that type of fuel and an exemption certificate applies in
    respect of the delivery in accordance with section 36.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When charge payable

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The charge under subsection (1) becomes payable at
  the later time referred to in that subsection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge not payable

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The charge under subsection (1) is not payable if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at the later time referred to in that subsection,
    the particular person is not a registered user;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a charge is payable under section 37 in respect of
    the fuel; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
    conditions are met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge --- ceasing to be registered
  user

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Part, if at any time fuel is
  delivered in a listed province to a particular person by a registered
  distributor in respect of that type of fuel, if the particular person
  is a registered user in respect of that type of fuel, if an exemption
  certificate applies in respect of the delivery in accordance with
  section 36 and if the Minister, at a later time, cancels the
  particular person's registration as a user, the particular person must
  pay to Her Majesty in right of Canada a charge in respect of the fuel
  and the listed province in the amount determined under section 40 to
  the extent that, at the later time, the fuel is held by the particular
  person in the listed province. The charge becomes payable at the later
  time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge not payable

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The charge under subsection (4) is not payable if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at the later time referred to in that subsection,
    the Minister registers the particular person as a distributor in
    respect of that type of fuel;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} at the later time referred to in that subsection,
    the particular person is a registered emitter, but only to the
    extent that, at the later time, the fuel is held at, or is in
    transit to, a covered facility of the particular person;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a charge is payable under section 37 in respect of
    the fuel; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
    conditions are met.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge --- diversion by a farmer

- **[[24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Part, if at any time
  fuel is delivered in a listed province by a registered distributor in
  respect of that type of fuel to a particular person that is a farmer
  and an exemption certificate applies in respect of the delivery in
  accordance with section 36, the particular person must pay to Her
  Majesty in right of Canada a charge in respect of the fuel and the
  listed province in the amount determined under section 40 to the
  extent that, at a later time, the fuel is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} used by the particular person in the listed
    province otherwise than in eligible farming activities; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} delivered by the particular person to another
    person unless the other person is a registered distributor in
    respect of that type of fuel and an exemption certificate applies in
    respect of the delivery in accordance with section 36.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When charge payable

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The charge under subsection (1) becomes payable at
  the later time referred to in that subsection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge not payable

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The charge under subsection (1) is not payable if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at the later time referred to in that subsection,
    the particular person is not a farmer;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a charge is payable under section 37 in respect of
    the fuel; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
    conditions are met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge --- ceasing to be a farmer

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Part, if at any time fuel is
  delivered in a listed province by a registered distributor in respect
  of that type of fuel to a particular person that is a farmer, if an
  exemption certificate applies in respect of the delivery in accordance
  with section 36 and if the particular person ceases, at a later time,
  to be a farmer, the particular person must pay to Her Majesty in right
  of Canada a charge in respect of the fuel and the listed province in
  the amount determined under section 40 to the extent that, at the
  later time, the fuel is held by the particular person in the listed
  province. The charge becomes payable at the later time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge not payable

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The charge under subsection (4) is not payable if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at the later time referred to in that subsection,
    the particular person is registered as a distributor in respect of
    that type of fuel;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} at the later time referred to in that subsection,
    the particular person is a registered emitter, but only to the
    extent that, at the later time, the fuel is held at, or is in
    transit to, a covered facility of the person;
