  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a charge is payable under section 37 in respect of
    the fuel; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
    conditions are met.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge --- delivery in a listed province

- **[[24.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Part, if at any time
  fuel is delivered in a listed province by a registered distributor in
  respect of that type of fuel to a particular person that is a fisher
  and an exemption certificate applies in respect of the delivery in
  accordance with section 36, the particular person must pay to Her
  Majesty in right of Canada a charge in respect of the fuel and the
  listed province in the amount determined under section 40 to the
  extent that, at a later time, the fuel is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} used by the particular person in the listed
    province otherwise than in eligible fishing activities; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} delivered by the particular person to another
    person unless the other person is a registered distributor in
    respect of that type of fuel and an exemption certificate applies in
    respect of the delivery in accordance with section 36.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When charge payable

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The charge under subsection (1) becomes payable at
  the later time referred to in that subsection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge not payable

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The charge under subsection (1) is not payable if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at the later time referred to in that subsection,
    the particular person is not a fisher;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a charge is payable under section 37 in respect of
    the fuel; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
    conditions are met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge --- ceasing to be a fisher

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this Part, if at any time fuel is
  delivered in a listed province by a registered distributor in respect
  of that type of fuel to a particular person that is a fisher, if an
  exemption certificate applies in respect of the delivery in accordance
  with section 36 and if the particular person ceases, at a later time,
  to be a fisher, the particular person must pay to Her Majesty in right
  of Canada a charge in respect of the fuel and the listed province in
  the amount determined under section 40 to the extent that, at the
  later time, the fuel is held by the particular person in the listed
  province. The charge becomes payable at the later time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge not payable

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The charge under subsection (4) is not payable if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at the later time referred to in that subsection,
    the particular person is registered as a distributor in respect of
    that type of fuel;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} at the later time referred to in that subsection,
    the particular person is a registered emitter, but only to the
    extent that, at the later time, the fuel is held at, or is in
    transit to, a covered facility of the person;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a charge is payable under section 37 in respect of
    the fuel; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
    conditions are met.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge --- combustible waste

**[[25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25 .sectionLabel}** Subject to this Part,
every person that, at a particular time, burns combustible waste in a
listed province for the purposes of producing heat or energy must pay to
Her Majesty in right of Canada a charge in respect of the combustible
waste and the listed province in the amount determined under section 41.
The charge becomes payable at the particular time.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge --- regulations

**[[26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-26 .sectionLabel}** Subject to this Part, a
prescribed person, a person of a prescribed class or a person meeting
prescribed conditions must pay to Her Majesty in right of Canada a
charge in respect of a type of fuel or combustible waste in the amount
determined in prescribed manner if prescribed circumstances exist or
prescribed conditions are met. The charge becomes payable at the
prescribed time.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge not payable --- regulations

**[[27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-27 .sectionLabel}** A charge under this Part
in respect of a type of fuel or combustible waste is not payable

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} by a prescribed person, a person of a prescribed
  class or a person meeting prescribed conditions; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
  conditions are met.

#### [SUBDIVISION B]{.HLabel3}[Application of Charge to Air, Marine, Rail and Road Carriers]{.HTitleText3} {#h-244480 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Net fuel quantity --- registered
specified air or marine carrier

**[[28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28 .sectionLabel}** The net fuel quantity of
a person that is a registered specified air carrier or registered
specified marine carrier in respect of a type of fuel for a reporting
period of the person, for that type of fuel and for a listed province is
the amount determined by the formula

::: {#244483 .Section}
A -- B

where

[A]{.dfn}
:   is the total of all quantities, each of which is a quantity of fuel
    of that type that is
    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} used by the person in the listed province during
      the reporting period other than a quantity of fuel of that type
      used in the listed province

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in a journey by aircraft or vessel, in a
        locomotive or in a specified commercial vehicle, or

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in a non-covered activity if the person is a
        registered user in respect of that type of fuel,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} used by the person in a covered air journey in
      respect of the listed province during the reporting period,

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} used by the person in a covered marine journey in
      respect of the listed province during the reporting period,

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} used by the person in a locomotive in the listed
      province during the reporting period,

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} used by the person in a specified commercial
      vehicle in the listed province during the reporting period,

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} delivered in the listed province by the person to
      another person during the reporting period unless

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the other person is a registered distributor in
        respect of that type of fuel and an exemption certificate
        applies in respect of the delivery in accordance with section
        36,

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the person and the other person are both
        registered specified air carriers in respect of that type of
        fuel and an exemption certificate applies in respect of the
        delivery in accordance with section 36, or

      - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the person and the other person are both
        registered specified marine carriers in respect of that type of
        fuel and an exemption certificate applies in respect of the
        delivery in accordance with section 36, or

    - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed quantity of fuel of that type, or a
      quantity of fuel of that type determined in prescribed manner, for
      the reporting period and the listed province; and

[B]{.dfn}
:   is
    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the total of all quantities, each of which is a
      prescribed quantity of fuel of that type, or a quantity of fuel of
      that type determined in prescribed manner, for the reporting
      period and the listed province, or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} unless paragraph (a) applies, zero.
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Net fuel quantity --- registered
specified rail carrier

**[[29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29 .sectionLabel}** The net fuel quantity of
a person that is a registered specified rail carrier in respect of a
type of fuel for a reporting period of the person, for that type of fuel
and for a listed province is the amount determined by the formula

::: {#244503 .Section}
A + B -- C

where

[A]{.dfn}
:   is the total of all quantities, each of which is a quantity of fuel
    of that type that is used by the person in a locomotive in the
    listed province during the reporting period estimated in a manner
    satisfactory to the Minister;

[B]{.dfn}
:   is the total of all quantities, each of which is a quantity of fuel
    of that type that is
    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} used by the person in the listed province during
      the reporting period other than a quantity of fuel of that type
      used in the listed province

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in a journey by aircraft or vessel, in a
        locomotive or in a specified commercial vehicle, or

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in a non-covered activity if the person is a
        registered user in respect of that type of fuel,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} used by the person in a covered air journey in
      respect of the listed province during the reporting period,

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} used by the person in a covered marine journey in
      respect of the listed province during the reporting period,

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} used by the person in a specified commercial
      vehicle in the listed province during the reporting period,

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} delivered in the listed province by the person to
      another person during the reporting period unless

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the other person is a registered distributor in
        respect of that type of fuel and an exemption certificate
        applies in respect of the delivery in accordance with section
        36, or

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the other person is a registered specified
        rail carrier in respect of that type of fuel and an exemption
        certificate applies in respect of the delivery in accordance
        with section 36, or

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed quantity of fuel of that type, or a
      quantity of fuel of that type determined in prescribed manner, for
      the reporting period and the listed province; and

[C]{.dfn}
:   is
    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the total of all quantities, each of which is a
      prescribed quantity of fuel of that type, or a quantity of fuel of
      that type determined in prescribed manner, for the reporting
      period and the listed province, or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} unless paragraph (a) applies, zero.
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Net fuel quantity --- registered air or
marine carrier

**[[30]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30 .sectionLabel}** The net fuel quantity of
a person that is a registered air carrier or registered marine carrier
in respect of a type of fuel for a reporting period of the person, for
that type of fuel and for a listed province is the amount determined by
the formula

::: {#244522 .Section}
A -- B

where

[A]{.dfn}
:   is the total of all quantities, each of which is a quantity of fuel
    of that type that is
    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} used by the person in a covered air journey in
      respect of the listed province during the reporting period,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} used by the person in a covered marine journey in
      respect of the listed province during the reporting period,

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} used by the person in a locomotive in the listed
      province during the reporting period,

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} used by the person in a specified commercial
      vehicle in the listed province during the reporting period,

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} removed from a supply tank of an aircraft,
      vessel, locomotive or specified commercial vehicle of the person
      at a location in the listed province during the reporting period,
      or

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed quantity of fuel of that type, or a
      quantity of fuel of that type determined in prescribed manner, for
      the reporting period and the listed province; and

[B]{.dfn}
:   is the total of all quantities, each of which is a quantity of fuel
    of that type that is
    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} transferred into a supply tank of an aircraft,
      vessel, locomotive or specified commercial vehicle of the person
      at a location in the listed province during the reporting period,
      or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed quantity of fuel of that type, or a
      quantity of fuel of that type determined in prescribed manner, for
      the reporting period and the listed province.
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Net fuel quantity --- registered rail
carrier

**[[31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31 .sectionLabel}** The net fuel quantity of
a person that is a registered rail carrier in respect of a type of fuel
for a reporting period of the person, for that type of fuel and for a
listed province is the amount determined by the formula

::: {#244536 .Section}
A + B -- C

where

[A]{.dfn}
:   is the total of all quantities, each of which is a quantity of fuel
    of that type that is used by the person in a locomotive in the
    listed province during the reporting period estimated in a manner
    satisfactory to the Minister;

[B]{.dfn}
:   is the total of all quantities, each of which is a quantity of fuel
    of that type that is
    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} used by the person in a covered air journey in
      respect of the listed province during the reporting period,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} used by the person in a covered marine journey in
      respect of the listed province during the reporting period,

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} used by the person in a specified commercial
      vehicle in the listed province during the reporting period,

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} removed from a supply tank of an aircraft,
      vessel, locomotive or specified commercial vehicle of the person
      at a location in the listed province during the reporting period,
      or

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed quantity of fuel of that type, or a
      quantity of fuel of that type determined in prescribed manner, for
      the reporting period and the listed province; and

[C]{.dfn}
:   is the total of all quantities, each of which is a quantity of fuel
    of that type that is
    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} transferred into a supply tank of an aircraft,
      vessel, locomotive or specified commercial vehicle of the person
      at a location in the listed province during the reporting period,
      or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed quantity of fuel of that type, or a
      quantity of fuel of that type determined in prescribed manner, for
      the reporting period and the listed province.
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Net fuel quantity --- registered road
carrier

**[[32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-32 .sectionLabel}** The net fuel quantity of
a person that is a registered road carrier in respect of a type of fuel
for a reporting period of the person, for that type of fuel and for a
listed province is the amount determined by the formula

::: {#244550 .Section}
A -- B

where

[A]{.dfn}
:   is the total of all quantities, each of which is a quantity of fuel
    of that type that is
    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} used by the person in a specified commercial
      vehicle in the listed province (unless the fuel is used at a
      covered facility of the person and the person is a registered
      emitter) during the reporting period,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} removed from a supply tank of a specified
      commercial vehicle of the person at a location in the listed
      province during the reporting period, or

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed quantity of fuel of that type, or a
      quantity of fuel of that type determined in prescribed manner, for
      the reporting period and the listed province; and

[B]{.dfn}
:   is the total of all quantities, each of which is a quantity of fuel
    of that type that is
    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} transferred into a supply tank of a specified
      commercial vehicle of the person at a location in the listed
      province (unless the location is a covered facility of the person
      and the person is a registered emitter) during the reporting
      period, or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed quantity of fuel of that type, or a
      quantity of fuel of that type determined in prescribed manner, for
      the reporting period and the listed province.
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Annual net fuel adjustment --- rail
carrier

**[[33]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-33 .sectionLabel}** If, at any time in a
particular calendar year, a person is a registered specified rail
carrier or registered rail carrier in respect of a type of fuel, the
annual net fuel adjustment of the person for the particular calendar
year, for that type of fuel and for a listed province is the amount
determined by the formula

::: {#244561 .Section}
A − B

where

[A]{.dfn}
:   is the total of all quantities, each of which is a quantity of fuel
    of that type that is
    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} used by the person in a locomotive in the listed
      province at any time in the particular calendar year when the
      person is a registered specified rail carrier or registered rail
      carrier in respect of that type of fuel, or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed quantity of fuel of that type, or a
      quantity of fuel of that type determined in prescribed manner, for
      the particular calendar year and the listed province; and

[B]{.dfn}
:   is the total of all quantities, each of which is a quantity of fuel
    of that type that is
    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the total determined for A in section 29 or 31
      for a reporting period of the person in the particular calendar
      year, for that type of fuel and for the listed province, or

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed quantity of fuel of that type, or a
      quantity of fuel of that type determined in prescribed manner, for
      the particular calendar year and the listed province.
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge --- net fuel quantity

**[[34]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-34 .sectionLabel}** If the net fuel quantity,
determined under any of sections 28 to 32, of a person for a reporting
period, for a type of fuel and for a listed province is a positive
amount, the person must pay to Her Majesty in right of Canada a charge
in respect of that net fuel quantity and the listed province in the
amount determined under section 40. The charge becomes payable on the
last day of the reporting period.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge --- annual net fuel adjustment

**[[35]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-35 .sectionLabel}** If the annual net fuel
adjustment, determined under section 33, of a person for a particular
calendar year, for that type of fuel and for a listed province is a
positive amount, the person must pay to Her Majesty in right of Canada a
charge in respect of that annual net fuel adjustment and the listed
province in the amount determined under section 40. The charge becomes
payable on June 30 of the calendar year following the particular
calendar year.

#### [SUBDIVISION C]{.HLabel3}[Exemption Certificate]{.HTitleText3} {#h-244573 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption certificate

- **[[36]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-36 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  fuel is delivered to a person by another person, an exemption
  certificate applies in respect of the delivery, for the purposes of
  this Part, only if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the certificate is made in prescribed form
    containing prescribed information;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the certificate includes a declaration by the
    person

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} that the person is a registered distributor in
      respect of that type of fuel,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} that the person is a registered specified air
      carrier in respect of that type of fuel,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} that the person is a registered specified
      marine carrier in respect of that type of fuel,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} that the person is a registered specified rail
      carrier in respect of that type of fuel,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} that the person is a registered emitter and that
      the fuel is for use at a covered facility of the person,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} that the person is a registered user in respect
      of that type of fuel and that the fuel is for use in a non-covered
      activity,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} that the person is a farmer, that the location
      at which the fuel is delivered is a farm, that the fuel is for use
      exclusively in the operation of eligible farming machinery or of
      an auxiliary component of eligible farming machinery and that all
      or substantially all of the fuel is for use in the course of
      eligible farming activities,

    - [(vii.1)]{.lawlabel} that the person is a fisher, that the fuel is
      for use exclusively in the operation of an eligible fishing vessel
      and that all or substantially all of the fuel is for use in the
      course of eligible fishing activities, or

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} that the person is a prescribed person, a
      person of a prescribed class or a person meeting prescribed
      conditions and that prescribed circumstances exist;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the person provides, in a manner satisfactory to
    the Minister, the certificate in respect of the delivery to the
    other person; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the other person retains the certificate and
    indicates to the person, in a manner satisfactory to the Minister,
    that the delivery is subject to the certificate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption certificate does not apply

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), if fuel is delivered to a
  fisher in a listed province that is not prescribed for the purposes of
  subparagraph 17(2)(a)(iii.1), an exemption certificate does not apply
  in respect of the delivery.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption certificate --- regulations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), if prescribed circumstances
  exist or prescribed conditions are met, an exemption certificate
  applies, for the purposes of this Part, in respect of a delivery of
  fuel in accordance with prescribed rules.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge --- false declaration

- **[[37]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  particular person delivers fuel in a listed province to another person
  at a particular time, if an exemption certificate applies in respect
  of the delivery in accordance with subsection 36(1) and if the
  declaration referred to in paragraph 36(1)(b) is, at the particular
  time, false, the following rules apply:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the other person must pay to Her Majesty in right
    of Canada a charge in respect of the fuel and the listed province in
    the amount determined under section 40;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the other person is liable to pay, in addition to
    any other penalty under this Part, a penalty equal to 25% of the
    amount of the charge under paragraph (a) payable in respect of the
    fuel; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the particular person knows, or ought to have
    known, that the declaration is, at the particular time, false, the
    particular person and the other person are jointly and severally, or
    solidarily, liable for the payment of the charge in respect of the
    fuel and the listed province under paragraph (a), the penalty under
    paragraph (b) and any related interest and penalties.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When charge payable

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The charge under subsection (1) becomes payable at
  the particular time referred to in that subsection.

#### [SUBDIVISION D]{.HLabel3}[Application of Charge in Special Circumstances]{.HTitleText3} {#h-244602 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge --- fuel held on adjustment day

- **[[38]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-38
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), every
  person that holds a quantity of a type of fuel in a listed province at
  the beginning of an adjustment day must pay to Her Majesty in right of
  Canada a charge in respect of the fuel and the listed province in the
  amount determined by the formula

  ::: {#244606 .Subsection}
  A -- B

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the amount that would be the amount of a charge in respect of
      the quantity of the fuel and the listed province determined under
      section 40 if that charge had become payable on the adjustment
      day; and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is
      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the adjustment day is commencement day,
        zero, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the amount that would be the
        amount of a charge in respect of the quantity of the fuel and
        the listed province determined under section 40 if that charge
        had become payable on the day before the adjustment day.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to a quantity of fuel
  held by a person if the fuel was delivered to the person by a
  registered distributor in respect of that type of fuel and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person is a registered emitter and the fuel is
    held at, or is in transit to, a covered facility of the person;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person is a registered user in respect of that
    type of fuel and an exemption certificate applies in respect of the
    delivery in accordance with section 36;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the person is a farmer, the fuel is a qualifying
    farming fuel, and an exemption certificate applies in respect of the
    delivery in accordance with section 36; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the person is a fisher, the fuel is a qualifying
    fishing fuel, and an exemption certificate applies in respect of the
    delivery in accordance with section 36.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When charge payable

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The charge under subsection (1) becomes payable on
  the adjustment day.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge not payable

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A charge under subsection (1) in respect of a
  quantity of a type of fuel held at the beginning of an adjustment day
  by a person in a listed province is not payable

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the person is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a registered distributor in respect of that type
      of fuel,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a registered specified air carrier in respect of
      that type of fuel,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a registered specified marine carrier in
      respect of that type of fuel, or

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a registered specified rail carrier in respect
      of that type of fuel;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the fuel is, in accordance with the [Ships' Stores
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-40), designated as ships'
    stores for use on board a conveyance of a class prescribed under
    those regulations; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the amount of the charge is less than \$1,000.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement to determine quantity of
  fuel

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Every person that holds fuel (other than in a supply
  tank of a vehicle) in a listed province at the beginning of an
  adjustment day and that is, or that can reasonably be expected to be,
  liable to pay a charge under this section in respect of the fuel and
  the listed province must determine the quantity of fuel of that type
  held in the listed province by that person at the beginning of the
  adjustment day.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge --- ceasing to be registered

- **[[39]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-39 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  person holds, at a particular time, a quantity of a type of fuel in a
  listed province, if the person was immediately before the particular
  time registered as a distributor, specified air carrier, specified
  marine carrier or specified rail carrier in respect of that type of
  fuel and if the Minister cancels that registration at the particular
  time, the person must pay to Her Majesty in right of Canada a charge
  in respect of the fuel and the listed province in the amount
  determined under section 40, unless the Minister also registers the
  person at the particular time as

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a distributor in respect of that type of fuel;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a specified air carrier in respect of that type of
    fuel;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a specified marine carrier in respect of that type
    of fuel; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a specified rail carrier in respect of that type of
    fuel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to fuel that is held by
  the person referred to in that subsection if that person is a
  registered emitter at the particular time but only to the extent that
  the fuel is, at the particular time, held by the person at, or is in
  transit to, a covered facility of the person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When charge payable

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The charge under subsection (1) becomes payable at
  the particular time referred to in that subsection.

#### [SUBDIVISION E]{.HLabel3}[Amount of Charge]{.HTitleText3} {#h-244642 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge amount --- fuel

- **[[40]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-40 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  amount of a charge payable under this Division (other than section 38)
  in respect of fuel and a listed province is equal to the amount
  determined by the formula

  ::: {#244646 .Subsection}
  A × B

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is
      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the charge becomes payable under section 34,
        the net fuel quantity,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the charge becomes payable under section 35,
        the annual net fuel adjustment, or

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the quantity of the fuel in
        respect of which the charge becomes payable; and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is
      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the charge becomes payable under section 35,
        the rate in respect of fuel of that type for the listed province
        applicable on December 31 of the calendar year that precedes the
        calendar year that includes the time at which the charge becomes
        payable, and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the rate in respect of fuel
        of that type for the listed province applicable at the time the
        charge becomes payable.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge amount --- mixture

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), if a manner is prescribed in
  respect of a mixture that is deemed to be fuel of a prescribed type
  under subsection 16(2), the amount of a charge payable under this
  Division in respect of such a mixture is equal to the amount
  determined in prescribed manner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge amount --- regulations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), if prescribed circumstances
  exist or prescribed conditions are met, the amount of a charge payable
  under this Division in respect of fuel and a listed province is equal
  to the amount determined in prescribed manner.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge amount --- combustible waste

- **[[41]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-41 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  amount of a charge payable under section 25 in respect of combustible
  waste and a listed province is equal to the amount determined by the
  formula

  ::: {#244662 .Subsection}
  A × B

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the quantity, expressed as a weight measured in tonnes, of the
      combustible waste; and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the rate in respect of combustible waste for the listed
      province applicable at the time the charge becomes payable.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge amount --- regulations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), if prescribed circumstances
  exist or prescribed conditions are met, the amount of a charge payable
  in respect of combustible waste and a listed province is equal to the
  amount determined in prescribed manner.

### [DIVISION 3]{.HLabel2}[Rebates]{.HTitleText2} {#h-244668 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Statutory recovery rights

**[[42]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42 .sectionLabel}** Except as specifically
provided under this Part, the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) or the
[Financial Administration Act](/eng/acts/F-11), no person has a right to
recover any money paid to Her Majesty in right of Canada as or on
account of, or that has been taken into account by Her Majesty in right
of Canada as, an amount payable under this Part.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rebate --- fuel removed from listed
province

- **[[43]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-43 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If at
  a particular time a person that is a registered emitter or is, in
  respect of that type of fuel, a registered user, a registered
  importer, registered air carrier, registered marine carrier or
  registered rail carrier removes a quantity of fuel of that type from a
  listed province, the Minister must pay to the person a rebate in
  respect of the quantity of fuel, the listed province and the reporting
  period of the person that includes the particular time if, at an
  earlier time in a particular reporting period,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person brought the quantity of fuel into the
    listed province from a place in Canada or imported the fuel at a
    location in the listed province, a charge under section 19 or 20 was
    payable by the person at the earlier time in respect of the fuel and
    the listed province and

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if that charge was payable under section 19 or
      subsection 20(2), that charge was taken into account in the
      determination of the net charge for the particular reporting
      period of the person, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if that charge was payable under subsection
      20(3), that charge was paid in accordance with subsection 20(4);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person removed the quantity of fuel from a
    covered facility of the person in the listed province and a charge
    under subsection 22(1) or (2)

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} became payable by the person at the earlier time
      in respect of the quantity of fuel and the listed province, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is taken into account in the determination of
      the net charge for the particular reporting period of the person;
      or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of fuel was held by the person at, or
    the quantity of fuel was in transit to, a facility or property of
    the person in the listed province that ceased, at the earlier time,
    to be a covered facility of the person and a charge under subsection
    22(4) or (5)

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} became payable by the person at the earlier time
      in respect of the quantity of fuel and the listed province, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is taken into account in the determination of
      the net charge for the particular reporting period of the person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amount of rebate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The amount of the rebate under subsection (1) is
  equal to the amount of the charge referred to in whichever of
  paragraphs (1)(a) to (c) applies.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rebate --- fuel brought to covered
facility

- **[[44]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-44 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If at
  a particular time a person is a registered emitter and brings a
  quantity of fuel to a covered facility of the person in a listed
  province for use at a covered facility of the person in the listed
  province, the Minister must pay to the person a rebate in respect of
  the fuel, the listed province and the reporting period of the person
  that includes the particular time if, at an earlier time in a
  particular reporting period,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person brought the quantity of fuel into the
    listed province from a place in Canada or imported the fuel at a
    location in the listed province, a charge under section 19 or 20 was
    payable by the person at the earlier time in respect of the quantity
    of fuel and the listed province and

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if that charge was payable under section 19 or
      subsection 20(2), that charge was taken into account in the
      determination of the net charge for the particular reporting
      period of the person, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if that charge was payable under subsection
      20(3), the charge was paid in accordance with subsection 20(4);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person removed the quantity of fuel from a
    covered facility of the person in the listed province, a charge
    under subsection 22(1) or (2) was payable by the person at the
    earlier time in respect of the quantity of fuel and the listed
    province and that charge was taken into account in the determination
    of the net charge for the particular reporting period of the person;
    or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of fuel was held by the person at, or
    the quantity of fuel was in transit to, a facility or property of
    the person in the listed province that ceased, at the earlier time,
    to be a covered facility of the person and a charge under subsection
    22(4) or (5)

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} became payable by the person at the earlier time
      in respect of the quantity of fuel and the listed province, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is taken into account in the determination of
      the net charge for the particular reporting period of the person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amount of rebate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The amount of the rebate under subsection (1) is
  equal to the amount of the charge referred to in whichever of
  paragraphs (1)(a) to (c) applies.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rebate --- fuel used in non-covered
activity

- **[[45]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-45 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  person, at a particular time in a reporting period of the person, is a
  registered user in respect of a type of fuel and the person uses a
  quantity of fuel of that type in a non-covered activity in a listed
  province, the Minister must pay to the person a rebate in respect of
  the fuel, the listed province and the reporting period if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person, at an earlier time in a particular
    reporting period of the person, brought the quantity of fuel into
    the listed province from a place in Canada or imported the fuel at a
    location in the listed province;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a charge under section 19 or 20 was payable by the
    person at the earlier time in respect of the quantity of fuel and
    the listed province and

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if that charge was payable under section 19 or
      subsection 20(2), that charge was taken into account in the
      determination of the net charge for the particular reporting
      period of the person, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if that charge was payable under subsection
      20(3), the charge was paid in accordance with subsection 20(4);
      and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the fuel is used at a location that is not a
    covered facility of the person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amount of rebate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The amount of the rebate under subsection (1) is
  equal to the amount of the charge referred to in paragraph (1)(b).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rebate --- net fuel quantity

- **[[46]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-46 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  net fuel quantity, determined under any of sections 28 to 32, of a
  person for a reporting period, for a type of fuel and for a listed
  province is a negative amount, the Minister must pay to the person a
  rebate in respect of that net fuel quantity, the listed province and
  the reporting period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amount of rebate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The amount of a rebate payable under subsection (1)
  is equal to the amount determined by the formula

  ::: {#244712 .Subsection}
  A × B

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the net fuel quantity referred to in that subsection; and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the rate in respect of fuel of that type for the listed
      province applicable on the last day of the reporting period
      referred to in that subsection.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amount of rebate --- regulations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (2), if prescribed circumstances
  exist or prescribed conditions are met, the amount of a rebate payable
  under this section is equal to the amount determined in prescribed
  manner.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rebate --- annual net fuel adjustment

- **[[47]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If an
  annual net fuel adjustment of a person, determined under section 33,
  for a calendar year, for a type of fuel and for a listed province is a
  negative amount, the Minister must pay to the person a rebate in
  respect of that annual net fuel adjustment and the listed province.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amount of rebate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The amount of a rebate payable under subsection (1)
  is equal to the amount determined by the formula

  ::: {#244723 .Subsection}
  A × B

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the annual net fuel adjustment referred to in that subsection;
      and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the rate in respect of fuel of that type for the listed
      province applicable on December 31 of the calendar year referred
      to in that subsection.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amount of rebate --- regulations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (2), if prescribed circumstances
  exist or prescribed conditions are met, the amount of a rebate payable
  under this section is equal to the amount determined in prescribed
  manner.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rebate --- regulations

**[[48]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-48 .sectionLabel}** The Minister must pay a
rebate in respect of fuel or combustible waste and a listed province to
a prescribed person, a person of a prescribed class or a person meeting
prescribed conditions in the amount determined in prescribed manner if
prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed conditions are met.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rebate --- payment in error

- **[[49]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-49 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister must pay a rebate to a person if the person paid an amount in
  excess of the amount that was payable by that person under this Part
  whether the amount was paid by mistake or otherwise.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amount of rebate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The amount of a rebate payable under subsection (1)
  by the Minister is the amount of the excess referred to in that
  subsection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on rebate

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A rebate under this section in respect of an amount
  must not be paid to a person to the extent that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amount was taken into account as an amount
    required to be paid by the person in respect of a reporting period
    of the person and the Minister has assessed the person for that
    period under section 108; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the amount was an amount assessed under section
    108.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for rebate

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Despite any other provision under this Part, a rebate
  under this section in respect of an amount is not to be paid to a
  person unless an application for the rebate is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} made in prescribed form containing prescribed
    information; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} filed with the Minister in prescribed manner within
    two years after the earlier of the day that the amount was taken
    into account in determining the net charge for a reporting period of
    the person and the day that the amount was paid to the Receiver
    General.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}One application per month

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Not more than one application for a rebate under this
  section may be made by a person in a calendar month.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on rebate

**[[50]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-50 .sectionLabel}** A rebate is not to be
paid to a person under this Division

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} to the extent that it can reasonably be regarded that
  the person has obtained or is entitled to obtain a rebate, refund or
  remission of the amount under any other section of this Act or under
  any other Act of Parliament; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
  conditions are met.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction on rebate

**[[51]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-51 .sectionLabel}** A rebate under this
Division is not to be paid to a person at any time unless all returns of
which the Minister has knowledge and that are required to be filed at or
before that time by the person under this Part, the [Excise Tax
Act](/eng/acts/E-15), the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3), the [Air
Travellers Security Charge Act](/eng/acts/A-10.5), the [Excise Act,
2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1), the [Underused Housing Tax
Act](/eng/acts/U-0.5) and the [Select Luxury Items Tax
Act](/eng/acts/S-8.35) have been filed with the Minister.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2018, c. 12, s. 186 "51"](#1350793-1352240){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1350793-1352240.html"}
- [2022, c. 5, s. 36](#1350793-1352241){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1350793-1352241.html"}
- [2022, c. 10, s. 168](#1356870-1374267){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1356870-1374267.html"}
- [2022, c. 10, s. 173](#1356944-1374283){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1356944-1374283.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-51-20220609.html "Link to previous version of section 51"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for rebate

**[[52]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-52 .sectionLabel}** Despite any other
provision under this Part, a rebate under this Division, other than
section 49, in respect of a particular reporting period of a person is
not to be paid unless an application for the rebate

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} is made in prescribed form containing prescribed
  information;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} unless paragraph (c) applies, is filed with the
  Minister in prescribed manner

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} on or before the day on or before which the return
    under section 69 is required to be filed for the last reporting
    period of the person that ends within two years after the end of the
    particular reporting period, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} with the return in respect of the reporting period
    in which the amount of the rebate is taken into account in
    determining the net charge for the reporting period; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the rebate is payable under section 47 in respect
  of an annual net fuel adjustment for a particular calendar year, is
  filed with the Minister in prescribed manner

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} on or before the day on which the return under
    section 69 is required to be filed for the reporting period of the
    person that includes June 30 of the year following the particular
    calendar year, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} with the return in respect of the reporting period
    that includes June 30 of the year following the particular calendar
    year.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Single application

**[[53]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-53 .sectionLabel}** Only one application may
be made under this Division for a rebate with respect to any matter.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction --- bankruptcy

**[[54]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-54 .sectionLabel}** If a trustee is appointed
under the [Bankruptcy and Insolvency Act](/eng/acts/B-3) to act in the
administration of the estate or succession of a bankrupt, a rebate under
this Part that the bankrupt was entitled to claim before the appointment
must not be paid after the appointment unless all returns required to be
filed in respect of the bankrupt under this Part, the [Excise Tax
Act](/eng/acts/E-15), the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3), the [Air
Travellers Security Charge Act](/eng/acts/A-10.5), the [Excise Act,
2001](/eng/acts/E-14.1), the [Underused Housing Tax
Act](/eng/acts/U-0.5) and the [Select Luxury Items Tax
Act](/eng/acts/S-8.35) in respect of periods ending before the
appointment have been filed and all amounts required under this Part and
those Acts to be paid by the bankrupt in respect of those periods have
been paid.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2018, c. 12, s. 186 "54"](#1350797-1352245){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1350797-1352245.html"}
- [2022, c. 5, s. 37](#1350797-1352246){.wb-lbx

### [DIVISION 4]{.HLabel2}[Registration, Reporting Periods, Returns and Payments]{.HTitleText2} {#h-244765 .Subheading}

#### [SUBDIVISION A]{.HLabel3}[Registration]{.HTitleText3} {#h-244766 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Distributor --- registration required

- **[[55]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-55 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person is required to be registered, for the purposes of this Part, as
  a distributor in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} both marketable natural gas and non-marketable
    natural gas, if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the person produces marketable natural gas or
      non-marketable natural gas in a listed province,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the person imports marketable natural gas or
      non-marketable natural gas at a location in a listed province
      otherwise than in a supply tank of a vehicle,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the person brings marketable natural gas or
      non-marketable natural gas into a listed province from a place in
      Canada otherwise than in a supply tank of a vehicle,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the person delivers marketable natural gas or
      non-marketable natural gas to another person in a listed province,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the person measures another person's consumption
      or usage, in a listed province, of marketable natural gas and

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the measurement is done on a regular basis and
        for the purpose of billing the other person or providing the
        other person's billing information to a third party, and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the marketable natural gas is delivered by way
        of a distribution system,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the person is a prescribed person, a person of a
      prescribed class or a person meeting prescribed conditions, or

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
      conditions are met; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a type of fuel other than marketable natural gas or
    non-marketable natural gas, if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the person produces fuel of that type in a listed
      province,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the person is a prescribed person, a person of a
      prescribed class or a person meeting prescribed conditions, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
      conditions are met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Timing of application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person that is required under subsection (1) to be
  registered as a distributor in respect of a type of fuel must apply to
  the Minister for registration before

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} unless paragraph (b) or (c) applies, the later of
    commencement day and

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the type of fuel is marketable natural gas or
      non-marketable natural gas, the day that is the earliest of

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the person first produces
        marketable natural gas or non-marketable natural gas in a listed
        province,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the person first imports
        marketable natural gas or non-marketable natural gas in a listed
        province otherwise than in a supply tank of a vehicle,

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the person first brings
        marketable natural gas or non-marketable natural gas into a
        listed province from a place in Canada otherwise than in a
        supply tank of a vehicle,

      - [(D)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the person first delivers
        marketable natural gas or non-marketable natural gas to another
        person in a listed province, and

      - [(E)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the person first measures
        another person's consumption or usage of marketable natural gas
        in a listed province if

        - [(I)]{.lawlabel} the measurement is for the purpose of billing
          the other person or providing the other person's billing
          information to a third party, and

        - [(II)]{.lawlabel} the marketable natural gas is delivered by
          way of a distribution system, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the fuel is not marketable natural gas and is
      not non-marketable natural gas, the day on which the person first
      produces fuel of that type in a listed province;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the person is a person prescribed under either
    subparagraph (1)(a)(vi) or (b)(ii), a person of a class prescribed
    under either of those subparagraphs or a person meeting conditions
    prescribed under either of those subparagraphs, the prescribed time;
    or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
    conditions are met, the prescribed time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Distributor --- registration permitted

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A person that is not required under subsection (1) to
  be registered as a distributor in respect of a type of fuel (other
  than marketable natural gas or non-marketable natural gas) may apply
  to the Minister to be registered, for the purposes of this Part, as a
  distributor in respect of that type of fuel if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person carries on the business of selling,
    delivering or distributing fuel of that type and, in the ordinary
    course of that business, delivers fuel of that type in a listed
    province

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} to another person for the purpose of resale, in
      the ordinary course of business, by the other person,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} to a registered distributor in respect of that
      type of fuel,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} to a farmer at a farm if the fuel is qualifying
      farming fuel,

    - [(iii.1)]{.lawlabel} to a fisher if the fuel is qualifying fishing
      fuel,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} to a registered specified air carrier in respect
      of that type of fuel if the fuel is qualifying aviation fuel,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} to a registered specified marine carrier in
      respect of that type of fuel if the fuel is qualifying marine
      fuel,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} to a registered specified rail carrier in
      respect of that type of fuel if the fuel is qualifying rail fuel,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} to a registered emitter at a covered facility
      of the registered emitter,

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} to a registered user in respect of that type
      of fuel, or

    - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} to another person if the fuel is, in accordance
      with the [Ships' Stores Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-40),
      designated as ships' stores for use on board a conveyance of a
      class prescribed under those regulations;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person carries on the business of selling,
    delivering or distributing fuel of that type and, in the ordinary
    course of that business, removes fuel of that type from a listed
    province;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the person is a prescribed person, a person of a
    prescribed class or a person meeting prescribed conditions; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
    conditions are met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} This section does not apply

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to a person in respect of a type of fuel if the
    person is, or is required to be, registered as

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a specified air carrier or air carrier in respect
      of that type of fuel,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a specified marine carrier or marine carrier in
      respect of that type of fuel, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a specified rail carrier or rail carrier in
      respect of that type of fuel;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to a prescribed person, a person of a prescribed
    class or a person meeting prescribed conditions; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
    conditions are met.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Importer --- registration required

- **[[56]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-56 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person is required to be registered, for the purposes of this Part, as
  an importer in respect of a type of fuel if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person imports fuel of that type --- other than
    fuel that is imported in a supply tank of a vehicle or fuel that is
    gasoline, kerosene, light fuel oil or propane in a quantity that
    does not exceed 200 L --- at a location in a listed province;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person brings fuel of that type --- other than
    fuel that is brought in a supply tank of a vehicle or fuel that is
    gasoline, kerosene, light fuel oil or propane in a quantity that
    does not exceed 200 L --- into a listed province from a place in
    Canada;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the person is a prescribed person, a person of a
    prescribed class or a person meeting prescribed conditions; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
    conditions are met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Timing of application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person that is required under subsection (1) to be
  registered as an importer in respect of a type of fuel must apply to
  the Minister for registration before

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} unless paragraph (b) or (c) applies, the later of
    commencement day and the earlier of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the person first imports fuel of
      that type --- other than fuel that is imported in a supply tank of
      a vehicle or fuel that is gasoline, kerosene, light fuel oil or
      propane in a quantity that does not exceed 200 L --- at a location
      in a listed province, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the person first brings fuel of
      that type --- other than fuel that is brought in a supply tank of
      a vehicle or fuel that is gasoline, kerosene, light fuel oil or
      propane in a quantity that does not exceed 200 L --- into a listed
      province from a place in Canada;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the person is a person prescribed under
    paragraph (1)(c), a person of a class prescribed under that
    paragraph or a person meeting conditions prescribed under that
    paragraph, the prescribed time; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
    conditions are met, the prescribed time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Importer --- registration permitted

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A person that is not required under subsection (1) to
  be registered as an importer in respect of a type of fuel, may apply
  to the Minister to be registered, for the purposes of this Part, as an
  importer in respect of that type of fuel if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person is an interjurisdictional rail carrier
    in respect of that type of fuel and

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the person, in the ordinary course of a business,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} imports, in a supply tank of a locomotive, fuel
        of that type at a location in a listed province,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} brings, in a supply tank of a locomotive, fuel
        of that type into a listed province from a place in Canada, or

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} removes, in a supply tank of a locomotive, fuel
        of that type from a listed province, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the person is not required to be registered
      under Division 4 of this Part, in respect of that type of fuel, as
      a specified air carrier, an air carrier, a specified marine
      carrier, a marine carrier, a specified rail carrier or a rail
      carrier;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person is a prescribed person, a person of a
    prescribed class or a person meeting prescribed conditions; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
    conditions are met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} This section does not apply

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to a person in respect of a type of fuel if the
    person is, or is required to be, registered as

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a distributor in respect of that type of fuel,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} as a specified air carrier or air carrier in
      respect of that type of fuel,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a specified marine carrier or marine carrier in
      respect of that type of fuel, or

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a specified rail carrier or rail carrier in
      respect of that type of fuel;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to a prescribed person, a person of a prescribed
    class or a person meeting prescribed conditions; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
    conditions are met.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Emitter --- registration permitted

- **[[57]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-57 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person may apply to the Minister to be registered, for the purposes of
  this Part, as an emitter if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person is, for the purposes Part 2, a person
    responsible for a covered facility and the person

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} has been issued a covered facility certificate in
      respect of the covered facility by the Minister of the Environment
      under section 171, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is not a prescribed person, a person of a
      prescribed class or a person meeting prescribed conditions;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person is a prescribed person, a person of a
    prescribed class or a person meeting prescribed conditions, in
    respect of a prescribed facility or property, a facility or property
    of a prescribed class or a facility or property meeting prescribed
    conditions; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
    conditions are met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} This section does not apply

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to a prescribed person, a person of a prescribed
    class or a person meeting prescribed conditions; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
    conditions are met.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}User of fuel --- registration permitted

- **[[58]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-58 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person that is not registered as a distributor in respect of a type of
  fuel and is not required to be so registered may apply to the Minister
  to be registered, for the purposes of this Part, as a user in respect
  of that type of fuel if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person uses, in the ordinary course of business
    of the person, fuel of that type in a non-covered activity in a
    listed province;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person is a prescribed person, a person of a
    prescribed class or a person meeting prescribed conditions; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
    conditions are met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} This section does not apply

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to a prescribed person, a person of a prescribed
    class or a person meeting prescribed conditions; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
    conditions are met.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}User of combustible waste ---
registration required

- **[[59]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-59 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person is required to be registered, for the purposes of this Part, as
  a user in respect of combustible waste if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person burns combustible waste in a listed
    province for the purpose of producing heat or energy;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person is a prescribed person, a person of a
    prescribed class or a person meeting prescribed conditions; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
    conditions are met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Timing of application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person that is required under subsection (1) to be
  registered as a user in respect of combustible waste must apply to the
  Minister for registration

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} unless paragraph (b) applies, before the later of
    commencement day and the day on which the person first burns
    combustible waste in a listed province for the purpose of producing
    heat or energy; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
    conditions are met, before the prescribed time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} This section does not apply

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to a prescribed person, a person of a prescribed
    class or a person meeting prescribed conditions; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
    conditions are met.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Air carrier --- registration required

- **[[60]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-60 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person (other than a registered emitter) that is an
  interjurisdictional air carrier in respect of a type of fuel that is a
  qualifying aviation fuel is required to be registered at a particular
  time for the purposes of this Part

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} unless paragraph (b) applies, as an air carrier in
    respect of that type of fuel if it can reasonably be expected that,
    during the calendar year that includes the particular time, all or
    substantially all of the fuel of that type used by the person will
    be used in vehicles and the greatest proportion of the fuel used in
    vehicles will be used in aircraft; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} as either a specified air carrier or an air carrier
    in respect of that type of fuel if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} during the calendar year that includes the
      particular time, all or substantially all of the fuel of that type
      used by the person is used in vehicles and the greatest proportion
      of the fuel used in vehicles is used in aircraft, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the person is a qualifying interjurisdictional
      air carrier in respect of that type of fuel throughout the
      calendar year that includes the particular time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Qualifying interjurisdictional air
  carrier --- previous year journeys

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subparagraph (1)(b)(ii), an
  interjurisdictional air carrier in respect of a type of fuel that is a
  qualifying aviation fuel is a qualifying interjurisdictional air
  carrier in respect of fuel of that type throughout a particular
  calendar year if the interjurisdictional air carrier completed
  journeys by aircraft in the calendar year that preceded the particular
  calendar year and the amount determined by the following formula is
  greater than, or equal to, 0.5:

  ::: {#244900 .Subsection}
  A/B

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the total of all quantities, each of which is a quantity of
      fuel of that type that was used by the interjurisdictional air
      carrier in an excluded air journey during the preceding calendar
      year; and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the total of all quantities, each of which is a quantity of
      fuel of that type that was used by the interjurisdictional air
      carrier in a covered air journey or an excluded air journey during
      the preceding calendar year.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Qualifying interjurisdictional air
  carrier --- no previous year journeys

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subparagraph (1)(b)(ii), a person
  that is, or that can reasonably be expected to be, an
  interjurisdictional air carrier in respect of a type of fuel that is a
  qualifying aviation fuel is a qualifying interjurisdictional air
  carrier in respect of fuel of that type throughout a particular
  calendar year if the person did not complete journeys by aircraft in
  the calendar year that preceded the particular calendar year and the
  amount determined by the following formula is greater than, or equal
  to, 0.5:

  ::: {#244906 .Subsection}
  A/B

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the total of all quantities, each of which is a quantity of
      fuel of that type that can reasonably be expected to be used by
      the person in an excluded air journey during the particular
      calendar year; and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the total of all quantities, each of which is a quantity of
      fuel of that type that can reasonably be expected to be used by
      the person in a covered air journey or an excluded air journey
      during the particular calendar year.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Air carrier --- registration required

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A person is required to be registered for the
  purposes of this Part

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} as a specified air carrier in respect of a type of
    fuel if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the person is a prescribed person, a person of a
      prescribed class or a person meeting prescribed conditions, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
      conditions are met; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} as an air carrier in respect of a type of fuel if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the person is a prescribed person, a person of a
      prescribed class or a person meeting prescribed conditions, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
      conditions are met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Air carrier --- registration permitted

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A person may apply to the Minister to be registered
  for the purposes of this Part

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} as a specified air carrier in respect of a type of
    fuel if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the person is a prescribed person, a person of a
      prescribed class or a person meeting prescribed conditions, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
      conditions are met; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} as an air carrier in respect of a type of fuel if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the person is a prescribed person, a person of a
      prescribed class or a person meeting prescribed conditions, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
      conditions are met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Timing of application

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A person that is required under this section to be
  registered as a specified air carrier or air carrier in respect of a
  type of fuel must apply to the Minister for registration

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} unless paragraph (b) applies, before the later of
    commencement day and the day that the person first meets the
    conditions under subsection (1); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
    conditions are met, before the prescribed time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} This section does not apply

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to a prescribed person, a person of a prescribed
    class or a person meeting prescribed conditions; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
    conditions are met.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Marine carrier --- registration required

- **[[61]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-61 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person (other than a registered emitter) that is an
  interjurisdictional marine carrier in respect of a type of fuel that
  is a qualifying marine fuel is required to be registered at a
  particular time for the purposes of this Part

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} unless paragraph (b) applies, as a marine carrier
    in respect of that type of fuel if it can reasonably be expected
    that, during the calendar year that includes the particular time,
    all or substantially all of the fuel of that type used by the person
    will be used in vehicles and the greatest proportion of the fuel
    used in vehicles will be used in vessels; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} as either a specified marine carrier or a marine
    carrier in respect of that type of fuel if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} during the calendar year that includes the
      particular time, all or substantially all of the fuel of that type
      used by the person is used in vehicles and the greatest proportion
      of the fuel used in vehicles is used in vessels, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the person is a qualifying interjurisdictional
      marine carrier in respect of that type of fuel throughout the
      calendar year that includes the particular time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Qualifying interjurisdictional marine
  carrier --- previous year journeys

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subparagraph (1)(b)(ii), an
  interjurisdictional marine carrier in respect of a type of fuel that
  is a qualifying marine fuel is a qualifying interjurisdictional marine
  carrier in respect of that type of fuel throughout a particular
  calendar year if the interjurisdictional marine carrier completed
  journeys by vessel in the calendar year that preceded the particular
  calendar year and the amount determined by the following formula is
  greater than, or equal to, 0.5:

  ::: {#244943 .Subsection}
  A/B

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the total of all quantities, each of which is a quantity of
      fuel of that type that is used by the interjurisdictional marine
      carrier in an excluded marine journey during the preceding
      calendar year; and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the total of all quantities, each of which is a quantity of
      fuel of that type that is used by the interjurisdictional marine
      carrier in a covered marine journey or an excluded marine journey
      during the preceding calendar year.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Qualifying interjurisdictional marine
  carrier --- no previous year journeys

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subparagraph (1)(b)(ii), a person
  that is, or that can reasonably be expected to be, an
  interjurisdictional marine carrier in respect of a type of fuel that
  is a qualifying marine fuel is a qualifying interjurisdictional marine
  carrier in respect of fuel of that type throughout a particular
  calendar year if the person did not complete journeys by vessel in the
  calendar year that preceded the particular calendar year and the
  amount determined by the following formula is greater than, or equal
  to, 0.5:

  ::: {#244949 .Subsection}
  A/B

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the total of all quantities, each of which is a quantity of
      fuel of that type that can reasonably be expected to be used by
      the person in an excluded marine journey during the particular
      calendar year; and

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the total of all quantities, each of which is a quantity of
      fuel of that type that can reasonably be expected to be used by
      the person in a covered marine journey or an excluded marine
      journey during the particular calendar year.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Marine carrier --- registration
  required

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A person is required to be registered for the
  purposes of this Part

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} as a specified marine carrier in respect of a type
    of fuel if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the person is a prescribed person, a person of a
      prescribed class or a person meeting prescribed conditions, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
      conditions are met; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} as a marine carrier in respect of a type of fuel if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the person is a prescribed person, a person of a
      prescribed class or a person meeting prescribed conditions, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
      conditions are met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Marine carrier --- registration
  permitted

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A person may apply to the Minister to be registered
  for the purposes of this Part

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} as a specified marine carrier in respect of a type
    of fuel if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the person is a prescribed person, a person of a
      prescribed class or a person meeting prescribed conditions, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
      conditions are met; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} as a marine carrier in respect of a type of fuel if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the person is a prescribed person, a person of a
      prescribed class or a person meeting prescribed conditions, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
      conditions are met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Timing of application

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A person that is required under this section to be
  registered as a specified marine carrier or marine carrier in respect
  of a type of fuel must apply to the Minister for registration

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} unless paragraph (b) applies, before the later of
    commencement day and the day that the person first meets the
    conditions under subsection (1); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
    conditions are met, before the prescribed time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} This section does not apply

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to a prescribed person, a person of a prescribed
    class or a person meeting prescribed conditions; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
    conditions are met.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rail carrier --- registration required

- **[[62]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-62 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person (other than a registered emitter) that is an
  interjurisdictional rail carrier in respect of a type of fuel that is
  a qualifying rail fuel is required to be registered at a particular
  time for the purposes of this Part, if it can reasonably be expected
  that, during the calendar year that includes the particular time, all
  or substantially all of the fuel of that type used by the person will
  be used in vehicles and the greatest proportion of the fuel used in
  vehicles will be used in locomotives

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} unless paragraph (b) applies, as a rail carrier in
    respect of that type of fuel; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} as either a specified rail carrier or rail carrier
    in respect of that type of fuel if the interjurisdictional rail
    carrier is a prescribed person, a person of a prescribed class or a
    person meeting prescribed conditions.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rail carrier --- registration required

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person is required to be registered for the
  purposes of this Part

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} as a specified rail carrier in respect of a type of
    fuel if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the person is a prescribed person, a person of a
      prescribed class or a person meeting prescribed conditions, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
      conditions are met; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} as a rail carrier in respect of a type of fuel if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the person is a prescribed person, a person of a
      prescribed class or a person meeting prescribed conditions, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
      conditions are met.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rail carrier --- registration permitted

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A person may apply to the Minister to be registered
  for the purposes of this Part

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} as a specified rail carrier in respect of a type of
    fuel if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the person is a prescribed person, a person of a
      prescribed class or a person meeting prescribed conditions, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} prescribed circumstances exist or prescribed
      conditions are met; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} as a rail carrier in respect of a type of fuel if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the person is a prescribed person, a person of a
